4.75.0, Engine 29.5.2 (linux/amd64), Compose v5.1.4, Git Bash 5.3.9 (MINGW64_NT-10.0-26200). Date 2026-10-23 (UTC), tester.
Why: the U4 proof (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4/windows-run.txt) went stale when docker-compose.sbom.yml changed again
(`tmpfs: - /tmp:mode=1777`, reviewer U4 B1) and scripts/bundle.sh, scripts/offline_check.sh, scripts/sbom_check.py changed in the
rework. Under A34 a compose-file change needs a recorded Windows run before the unit commits. Required by the orchestrator:
2b the sbom config resolves on Windows with tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777; 3b bundle.sh writes 6 valid SBOMs with NO "unable to get
filesystem cache" line (count 0), unlicensed counts ke-app 19, ke-gateway 0, neo4j 28, phoenix 35, postgres 25, valkey 1, and the
drift check prints its summary line.
Hashed files: 28, listed in <scratch>/out-u4r1/files.txt (copied at the end): every A34-scoped file (docker/** 13 files,
docker-compose*.yml 6, scripts/*.ps1 4) plus the U4 scripts (scripts/bundle.sh, scripts/offline_check.sh, scripts/sbom_check.py;
docker/source-policy.json is under docker/) plus uv.lock and pyproject.toml. sha256 recorded at start (Git Bash 00-start; and
section 0, PowerShell) and at the end (final check, diff against 00-start): identical, so the proof is tied to this exact tree.
Template: docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4/windows-run.txt -- same driver, same shim, same jq wrapper, same phases and order (pre,
build, 3b from Git Bash, up, down, tests; then verify fast and the final check from Git Bash). The r1 driver and Git Bash scripts
were derived from the U4 ones by a tester edit script whose every replacement had to apply; they are copied in full at the end.
Differences from the U4 run, on purpose:
  - own project COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4r1win and own image tag KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4r1win-464fa11 (built in phase build from
    this tree, from the BuildKit cache in about 4 s; removed in section 8); the 'local' tag was never used or moved;
  - .env = .env.example plus one comment marker line (U4R1WIN-THROWAWAY; no real keys; KE_MODEL_UPSTREAM empty); .env did not
    exist before (00-start, section 1) and was removed after (section 8, final check);
  - 28 files hashed (U4: 8);
  - section 2b: the tmpfs check now reads the entry as '<path>:<options>': the resolved tmpfs is exactly @('/tmp:mode=1777')
    (ordinal), mount path /tmp, options mode=1777, and XDG_CACHE_HOME=/tmp/cache sits under that mount path (U4's check compared
    the whole entry and would read False for '/tmp:mode=1777');
  - section 3b: NEW Git Bash probe: a real sbom container created by compose (create --pull never, never started, then removed,
    project ke-tester-u4r1win-diag) shows HostConfig.Tmpfs {"/tmp":"mode=1777"}; the cache-warning step counts the line
    "unable to get filesystem cache" (must be 0) in the bundle.sh output and, separately, in the drift base-SBOM run, whose
    output is now tee'd to <scratch>/out-u4r1/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt; NEW expected-count compare; the drift checker carries
    --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-sbomcheck, as offline_check.sh's now carries ${project}-sbomcheck
    (U4's 3b ran it without a label);
  - TESTER-SCRIPT BUG, recorded not hidden: the 3b expected-count compare used a sed pattern anchored on ': <n>$', but each header
    line ends with the by-type breakdown, e.g. ': 19 (deb: 18, generic: 1)', so sed left the lines unchanged and diff exited 1
    (3b shows it). The six header lines and the host recount in the same 3b already show the expected numbers; the compare was
    re-run with the pattern fixed in "3b addendum" below, on the same raw bundle.sh output (sha256 shown), and is equal;
  - section 3c (U4's diagnostic of why syft could not create /tmp/cache) dropped: the fix it supported is now in the repo, and 3b
    shows its effect;
  - bundle.sh `docker tag`s ke-app/ke-gateway:bundle-464fa1165504 (the developer's, 96702e57... and e057c9f4...) to the tester's
    images; 3b put both back on their earlier IDs and the final check shows them unchanged; the ke-vendor tags were re-applied to
    the same IDs (no change);
  - jq: as U3/U4, bundle.sh ran with <scratch>/bin/jq on PATH (the winget jq.exe with -b, LF output).
Parallel work: reviewer and security ran read-only checks at the same time; this run touched only its own project, tag, scratch
dirs and the throwaway .env; the final check lists the shared state (bundle tags, base image, syft image) as before.

==================== 3b-drift. U4 (ruling 6; PSC-14/22, N7, N10 B3): the image-side drift check against this run's ke-app SBOM, the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it ====================

Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after bundle.sh), with its variables set the same way; the one difference: the drift work dir is
made in <scratch> (mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX) rather than the repo's dist/, so this run writes nothing in the repo. The base pull
output is shown here (offline_check.sh sends its stdout to /dev/null). The SBOM run inherits COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME (ke-tester-u4r1win), as
offline_check's inherits ke-offline-<random> (ruling 7). The checker carries --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-sbomcheck, as
offline_check.sh's carries ${project}-sbomcheck.

# U4 rework r1 (tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777, review B1) Windows proof driver (tester): the U4 driver (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u4/windows-run.txt) with project, tag, marker,
# hash list (all A34-scoped files plus the U4 scripts, from <scratch>\out-u4r1\files.txt) and the 2b tmpfs checks changed. That driver was itself the U3 driver (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u3/windows-run.txt) with project, tag, marker and hash list changed
# (docker/source-policy.json added), section 2b extended again (XDG_CACHE_HOME resolves to /tmp/cache on the tmpfs); sections 4b and 4c kept. Section 3b (bundle.sh) runs from Git Bash between phases build and up.
# Own project and image tag so no other project or image is touched.
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][ValidateSet('pre', 'build', 'up', 'down', 'tests')][string]$Phase)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$PSStyle.OutputRendering = 'PlainText'
$repo = 'C:\repos\Knowledge-engine'
$scratch = Split-Path -Parent $PSCommandPath
$shimDir = Join-Path $scratch 'shim'
$envBak = Join-Path $scratch 'env.bak'
$project = 'ke-tester-u4r1win'
$tag = 'u4r1win-464fa11'
Set-Location $repo
$env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME = $project
$env:KE_IMAGE_TAG = $tag
$filter = "label=com.docker.compose.project=$project"

function Section([string]$t) { ''; "==================== $t ===================="; '' }
function Step([string]$label, [scriptblock]$sb) {
    "PS> $label"
    $global:LASTEXITCODE = $null
    & $sb 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { "$_" }
    "[exit code: $global:LASTEXITCODE]"
    ''
}
function EnvDiff {
    Compare-Object (Get-Content .env.example) (Get-Content .env) |
        ForEach-Object { '{0} {1}' -f $(if ($_.SideIndicator -eq '=>') { '+ .env        ' } else { '- .env.example' }), $_.InputObject }
}
function ProjectPorts {
    docker ps --filter $filter --format '{{.Names}}  {{.Ports}}'
}

$pyMaps = 'import importlib.util as u, psycopg; psycopg.pq.version(); print("psycopg_c.pq:", u.find_spec("psycopg_c.pq").origin); print("libpq mapped:", sorted({l.split()[-1] for l in open("/proc/self/maps") if "libpq" in l})); print("psycopg_binary spec:", u.find_spec("psycopg_binary"))'
$pyPip = "import importlib.util as u; print(u.find_spec('pip'))"
$shLdd = 'for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done'

        $c = [Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new()
        $ok = $false
        try { $ok = $c.ConnectAsync($ip, 47100).Wait(3000) -and $c.Connected } catch { $ok = $false } finally { $c.Dispose() }

    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyImpl`" (worker shares ke-app)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyImpl }

    Section "4c. (PSC-14, N10a/U1, repeated): no pip in the launched api; flattened ke-app (project $project)"
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c $pyPip }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (no pip wheel)' { docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (same, long form)' { docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyPip`" (worker shares ke-app; must print None)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker image history ke-app:$tag" { docker image history "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' ke-app:$tag" { docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image inspect ke-app:$tag --format '<RootFS layer count>; <Config.Env>'" { docker image inspect "ke-app:$tag" --format '{{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers; Env={{json .Config.Env}}' }

    Section '5. Safety shim verification (before the refusal case)'
    $shimLog = Join-Path $scratch 'shim.log'
    if (Test-Path $shimLog) { Remove-Item $shimLog }
    $env:KE_SHIM_LOG = $shimLog
    $realPath = $env:PATH
    $env:PATH = "$shimDir;$realPath"
    Step 'shim: Get-Command docker (resolves to the shim)' { (Get-Command docker).Source -replace [regex]::Escape($scratch), '<scratch>' }
    Step "shim: docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' (passes through)" { docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' }
    Step 'shim: docker compose up --help (must be intercepted, exit 99; harmless if not)' { docker compose up --help | Select-Object -First 2 }
    Step 'shim call log' { Get-Content $shimLog }
    Remove-Item $shimLog

    Section '5a. Refusal: KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0 and KE_ENV=prod in .env (launch.ps1 under the shim)'
    (Get-Content .env) -replace '^KE_BIND_ADDRESS=.*$', 'KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0' -replace '^KE_ENV=.*$', 'KE_ENV=prod' | Set-Content .env
    Step 'diff .env.example .env' { EnvDiff }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 (under the shim)' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'shim call log (no compose up)' { Get-Content $shimLog; ''; "compose up reached: $([bool](Get-Content $shimLog | Where-Object { $_ -match '\bcompose\b' -and $_ -match '\bup\b' }))" }
    $env:PATH = $realPath
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SHIM_LOG
    Step 'project ports (unchanged, 127.0.0.1 only)' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Restore .env from <scratch>\env.bak; diff .env.example .env' { Copy-Item $envBak .env -Force; EnvDiff }
}
'down' {
    Section '6. scripts\teardown.ps1 (no -Volumes: containers and networks go, named volumes stay)'
    Step "volumes before: docker volume ls --filter $filter" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 }
    Step "docker ps -a --filter $filter" { docker ps -a --filter $filter }
    Step "docker network ls --filter $filter" { docker network ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter $filter (kept)" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }

    Section '7. scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes (the tester removes its own project volumes)'
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes }
    Step "docker ps -a --filter $filter" { docker ps -a --filter $filter }
    Step "docker network ls --filter $filter" { docker network ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter $filter" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter name=${project}_" { docker volume ls --filter "name=${project}_" }
    Step "docker compose ls -a --filter name=$project" { docker compose ls -a --filter "name=$project" }

    Section '8. Remove temporary .env and the tester-owned image tags'
    Step 'Remove-Item .env, <scratch>\env.bak; Test-Path .env' { Remove-Item .env, $envBak; Test-Path .env }
    Step "docker image rm ke-app:$tag ke-gateway:$tag ke-gates:$tag (untag only; tester-owned tags)" { docker image rm "ke-app:$tag" "ke-gateway:$tag" "ke-gates:$tag" }
    Step "docker image ls --filter reference=ke-*:$tag" { docker image ls --filter "reference=ke-*:$tag" }
    Step 'git status --short' { git status --short }
}
'tests' {
    Section '9. Windows-only tests (skipped in the Linux gates image; run on this host)'
    $env:HARNESS_TRACING = 'off'
    Remove-Item Env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME, Env:KE_IMAGE_TAG
    Step '.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest -v tests.test_offline_bundle.InstallPs1WindowsAclTest tests.test_stack_scripts.LaunchPs1WindowsEnvTest' {
        & .venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest -v tests.test_offline_bundle.InstallPs1WindowsAclTest tests.test_stack_scripts.LaunchPs1WindowsEnvTest
    }
}
}

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/docker.cmd ====================
@pwsh -NoProfile -File "%~dp0shim.ps1" %*
@exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/shim.ps1 ====================
# Safety shim (tester, U3 Windows proof; copied from the U2 shim): logs each docker call, refuses any `docker compose ... up` with exit 99, passes the rest to the real docker.exe.
if ($env:KE_SHIM_LOG) { Add-Content -Path $env:KE_SHIM_LOG -Value ($args -join ' ') }
if (($args -contains 'compose') -and ($args -contains 'up')) { Write-Output 'shim: intercepted docker compose up (exit 99)'; exit 99 }
& 'C:\Program Files\Docker\Docker\resources\bin\docker.exe' @args
exit $LASTEXITCODE

exec "<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe" -b "$@"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bundle-u4r1.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Section 3b (tester, U4 rework r1 Windows proof): the U4 section 3b script with project/tag/out dir changed, a Git Bash
# tmpfs probe on a created sbom container, the cache-warning count that must be 0, the expected unlicensed counts, and
# the drift checker's --label as offline_check.sh now passes it. The U4 script's own header follows.
# Section 3b (tester, U4 Windows proof): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this host, to a scratch --out
# (the U3 section 3b script, project/tag changed), then the U4 additions: the per-image unlicensed report lines,
# the syft /tmp/cache warning, and the image-side drift check run the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it
# (pull the base by digest, docker save, base SBOM through docker-compose.sbom.yml, sbom_check.py drift in ke-app).
set -u
S=<scratch>
R=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine
SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")"; SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$SM" RED_SW="$SW"
red() { perl -pe 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#C:\\Users\\adam#<profile>#gi'; }
run() { echo "\$ $1" | red; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
sec() { echo; echo "==================== $1 ===================="; echo; }
cd "$R"
export COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u4r1win KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4r1win-464fa11
export PATH="$S/bin:$PATH"
OUT="$S/bundle-u4r1win-amd64"
SHA12="$(git rev-parse HEAD | cut -c1-12)"
RAW="$S/out-u4r1/03b-bundle-raw.txt"
project=ke-tester-u4r1win
export S R OUT RAW project

sec "3b. (PSC-13/U3, repeated; U4: unlicensed report, image-side drift check; U4 rework: tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777, no syft cache warning, expected counts): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this Windows host, to a scratch --out"
echo "date (UTC): $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
echo "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME KE_IMAGE_TAG=$KE_IMAGE_TAG (exported, as build.ps1 tagged the images in phase build); --out <scratch>/bundle-u4r1win-amd64"
echo
run 'bash --version | head -1; uname -s -r; command -v cygpath docker'
echo '--- jq: bundle.sh needs it on PATH; the host has jq 1.8.2 from winget, not on the Git Bash PATH, and native jq.exe writes CRLF:'
run 'J=<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe; "$J" --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | "$J" -er .a | od -c | head -1; sha256sum "$J"'
echo '--- so <scratch>/bin/jq (on PATH for this section only) runs it with -b, LF output (as in U3):'
run 'cat "$(command -v jq)"; command -v jq; jq --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | jq -er .a | od -c | head -1'
run 'echo "id -u:id -g = $(id -u):$(id -g) (the KE_SBOM_USER bundle.sh passes)"'

echo '--- fail-closed from Git Bash (U3 L-2, repeated): KE_SBOM_USER empty, and unset; then set, with XDG_CACHE_HOME in the resolved environment (U4)'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER= docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'env -u KE_SBOM_USER KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json | jq -c ".services.sbom | {user, tmpfs, XDG_CACHE_HOME: .environment.XDG_CACHE_HOME, TMPDIR: .environment.TMPDIR}"'

echo '--- U4 rework (review B1) from Git Bash: the tmpfs options compose gives a real sbom container (created with --pull never, never started, then removed)'
run 'D="$S/tmpfs-probe-r1"; mkdir -p -- "$D/in" "$D/out"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(cygpath -m "$D/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(cygpath -m "$D/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose -p ke-tester-u4r1win-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml create --pull never sbom; id=$(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-diag); docker inspect --format "State={{.State.Status}} User={{.Config.User}} Tmpfs={{json .HostConfig.Tmpfs}} ReadonlyRootfs={{.HostConfig.ReadonlyRootfs}} NetworkMode={{.HostConfig.NetworkMode}}" "$id"; docker inspect --format "{{range .Config.Env}}{{println .}}{{end}}" "$id" | grep -E "^(XDG_CACHE_HOME|TMPDIR)="; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(cygpath -m "$D/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(cygpath -m "$D/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose -p ke-tester-u4r1win-diag --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml rm -f sbom; echo "left: $(docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-diag | wc -l)"; rm -rf -- "$D"'

echo '--- before'
run "ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"  # must not exist"
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs before: the U3 location (TMPDIR), then the U4 one beside --out (review N6) plus drift work dirs'
run 'docker compose -f docker-compose.yml --env-file .env.example config --format json | jq -r ".services|to_entries[]|\"\(.key) \(.value.image)\"" | sort  # the refs bundle.sh will save'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{.Id}}' ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11 ke-gateway:u4r1win-464fa11"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/|^anchore/syft' | sort  # tags bundle.sh (re)points; restored after if moved"
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt"
run 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Names}} {{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u4r1win"  # containers of ke-tester-u4r1win* projects (expect 0: stack not up yet)'

echo '--- freshness (security M-1): bundle.sh uses KE_IMAGE_TAG=u4r1win-464fa11, the tag phase build just built from this tree'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} id={{.Id}} created={{.Created}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}' ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11 ke-gateway:u4r1win-464fa11"
APPID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11)"; GWID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-gateway:u4r1win-464fa11)"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E ' ($APPID|$GWID)\$'  # every tag on the two fresh image IDs"
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-probe --entrypoint python ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11 -I -c \"import importlib.util as u; print('psycopg_binary spec:', u.find_spec('psycopg_binary')); print('pip spec:', u.find_spec('pip')); import psycopg; print('psycopg impl:', psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())\""
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-probe --entrypoint sh ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11 -c 'dpkg-query -W libpq5; ls -d /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/psycopg_binary* 2>&1 | head -3'"

echo '--- the run (full output; time in seconds after it; a raw copy is kept for the greps below)'
echo "\$ scripts/bundle.sh --out <scratch>/bundle-u4r1win-amd64"
t0=$(date +%s)
scripts/bundle.sh --out "$OUT" 2>&1 | red | tee "$RAW"
rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
echo "[exit code: $rc] [elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"
echo

echo '--- U4: the per-image unlicensed report lines bundle.sh printed (sbom_check.py finish, stderr), in run order'
run 'grep "^sbom_check: " "$RAW"'
run 'grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW"  # the six header lines'
echo '--- U4 rework (review B1): syft cache warnings in the bundle.sh run (XDG_CACHE_HOME=/tmp/cache on tmpfs /tmp:mode=1777); the first count must be 0'
run 'echo "unable to get filesystem cache: $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$RAW")"; echo "WARN lines: $(grep -c "WARN" "$RAW")"; echo "lines naming /tmp/cache: $(grep -c "/tmp/cache" "$RAW")"; echo "lines naming /.cache/syft: $(grep -c "/.cache/syft" "$RAW")"; echo "permission denied: $(grep -ci "permission denied" "$RAW")"'
run 'grep "WARN" "$RAW" | sort | uniq -c  # every distinct WARN line (none expected)'
echo '--- U4 rework: the per-image unlicensed counts against the expected ones (ke-app 19, ke-gateway 0, neo4j 28, phoenix 35, postgres 25, valkey 1)'
run 'printf "%s\n" "ke-app 19" "ke-gateway 0" "neo4j 28" "phoenix 35" "postgres 25" "valkey 1" > "$S/out-u4r1/expected-counts.txt"; grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW" | sed -E "s/^sbom_check: ([a-z0-9-]+): unlicensed packages.*: ([0-9]+)$/\1 \2/" | sort > "$S/out-u4r1/printed-counts.txt"; cat "$S/out-u4r1/printed-counts.txt"; diff "$S/out-u4r1/expected-counts.txt" "$S/out-u4r1/printed-counts.txt" && echo "printed counts == expected"'

echo '--- the six SBOMs (and the image tars they describe)'
run "cd \"$OUT\" && ls -la app/sboms && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' app/sboms/* && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' images/*"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && cat bundle.json"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && grep -c '' MANIFEST.sha256 && grep 'app/sboms/' MANIFEST.sha256"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && sha256sum -c --quiet MANIFEST.sha256 && echo 'MANIFEST.sha256: every file verifies'"
run "cat \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; (cd \"$S\" && sha256sum bundle-u4r1win-amd64/MANIFEST.sha256)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/check_sboms.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R")\"  # validate() per file, the tests' equivalent (U3 helper, unchanged)"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/recount_unlicensed.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\"  # host recount with sbom_check.unlicensed_report on the written files (must equal the printed counts)"

sec "3b-drift. U4 (ruling 6; PSC-14/22, N7, N10 B3): the image-side drift check against this run's ke-app SBOM, the way scripts/offline_check.sh runs it"
echo "Commands are offline_check.sh's own (host half, after bundle.sh), with its variables set the same way; the one difference: the drift work dir is"
echo "made in <scratch> (mktemp -d <scratch>/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX) rather than the repo's dist/, so this run writes nothing in the repo. The base pull"
echo "output is shown here (offline_check.sh sends its stdout to /dev/null). The SBOM run inherits COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME (ke-tester-u4r1win), as"
echo "offline_check's inherits ke-offline-<random> (ruling 7). The checker carries --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-sbomcheck, as"
echo "offline_check.sh's carries \${project}-sbomcheck."
echo
run 'grep -n -E "^FROM .* AS runtime$|^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=" docker/app.Dockerfile'
base_ref="$(sed -n 's/^FROM \([^ ]*\) AS runtime$/\1/p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
libpq_pin="$(sed -n 's/^ARG LIBPQ_VERSION=//p' docker/app.Dockerfile)"
app_ref="$(sed -n 's/.*"ref": "\(ke-app:[^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$OUT/bundle.json" | head -n 1)"
export base_ref libpq_pin app_ref
run '[[ "$base_ref" =~ ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._/-]*:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]] && echo "base_ref=$base_ref (passes the offline_check.sh pin regex)"; echo "libpq_pin=$libpq_pin"; echo "app_ref=$app_ref (from bundle.json)"'
echo '--- the base in the image store before the pull (present: an earlier offline_check run pulled it; not removed here, a shared image)'
run 'docker image ls --digests --no-trunc --format "{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.Digest}} {{.ID}} {{.CreatedSince}}" python | grep "${base_ref##*@}"; docker image inspect --format "{{.Id}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}" "$base_ref"'
echo '--- pull by digest (offline_check: docker pull "$base_ref" > /dev/null || die ...; one call, no retry)'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker pull "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[pull elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc'
DW="$(mktemp -d "$S/.ke-drift.XXXXXXXX")"; export DW
run 'echo "drift_work=$DW"; mkdir -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && chmod 0700 -- "$DW/in" "$DW/out" "$DW/out/tmp" && ls -la "$DW" "$DW/out"'
run 't0=$(date +%s); docker save -o "$DW/in/base.tar" "$base_ref"; rc=$?; echo "[save elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/in/base.tar"; exit $rc'
echo '--- the base SBOM (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim; hostpath = cygpath -m); its WARN lines are part of this output'
run '( hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; t0=$(date +%s); MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 KE_SBOM_IN="$(hostpath "$DW/in")" KE_SBOM_OUT="$(hostpath "$DW/out")" KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml run --rm --pull never sbom docker-archive:/in/base.tar --scope all-layers -o spdx-json=/out/raw.spdx.json >&2; rc=$?; echo "[sbom elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"; exit $rc ) 2>&1 | tee "$S/out-u4r1/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt"; exit ${PIPESTATUS[0]}'
run 'echo "base SBOM run: unable to get filesystem cache: $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$S/out-u4r1/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt"); WARN lines: $(grep -c "WARN" "$S/out-u4r1/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt")"'
run 'stat -c "%s bytes  %n" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"; jq -r "\"spdxVersion=\(.spdxVersion) name=\(.name) packages=\(.packages|length)\"" "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json"'
echo '--- sbom_check.py drift in the ke-app image (offline_check.sh lines, verbatim)'
DRIFT='hostpath() { if command -v cygpath > /dev/null 2>&1; then cygpath -m -- "$1"; else printf "%s" "$1"; fi; }; repo_host="$(hostpath "$PWD")"; MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --read-only --cap-drop ALL --security-opt no-new-privileges --pull never --user "$(id -u):$(id -g)" --label "com.docker.compose.project=${project}-sbomcheck" -v "$repo_host/scripts/sbom_check.py:/opt/ke/sbom_check.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py:/opt/ke/added.py:ro" -v "$repo_host/licenses:/opt/ke/licenses:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$OUT/app/sboms"):/sboms:ro" -v "$(hostpath "$DW/out"):/base:ro" --entrypoint python "$app_ref" -I /opt/ke/sbom_check.py drift'
LIC='--licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-incorporated.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-HACL.txt --licence /opt/ke/licenses/CPython-3.12-KaRaMeL.txt'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC && echo \"offline: image-side drift check: the ke-app SBOM matches its base (\$base_ref)\""
echo '--- what the checker compared (tester, host side, sbom_check.py loaded by path; informational)'
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/drift_inputs.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/scripts/sbom_check.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT/app/sboms/ke-app.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$DW/out/raw.spdx.json")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R/tests/test_notice_ke_app.py")\""
echo '--- negative controls (tester): the same docker run, one input wrong each; both must exit 1 naming the problem'
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /base/raw.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq 15.18-0+deb12u1 --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 1: wrong libpq pin"
run "$DRIFT --sbom /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --base /sboms/ke-app.spdx.json --added-from /opt/ke/added.py --libpq \"\$libpq_pin\" --lock /app/uv.lock $LIC  # control 2: the ke-app SBOM as its own base (adds no Debian package)"
echo '--- drift cleanup (offline_check: rm -rf -- "$drift_work")'
run 'rm -rf -- "$DW"; ls -d "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l'

echo '--- after: nothing left by the run'
run 'docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-sbom -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-sbomcheck -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win-notices -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u4r1win -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter ancestor=ke-app:u4r1win-464fa11 -q | wc -l  # sbom, sbomcheck, notices; the drift SBOM run (project ke-tester-u4r1win); the drift checker (no label: ancestor ke-app)'
run 'docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.service=sbom | wc -l  # sbom containers, any project'
run 'docker compose ls -a --filter name=ke-tester-u4r1win'
run 'docker network ls --filter name=ke-tester-u4r1win'
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l; ls -d "$S"/.ke-sbom.* "$S"/.ke-drift.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs left (trap removes; expect the counts from before)'
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/out-u4r1/tags-after.txt"
run "diff \"$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt\" \"$S/out-u4r1/tags-after.txt\" && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: unchanged'"
if ! diff -q "$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt" "$S/out-u4r1/tags-after.txt" > /dev/null; then
  echo '--- restoring moved tags to their earlier image IDs'
  while read -r t id; do run "docker tag $id $t"; done < "$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt"
  comm -13 "$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt" "$S/out-u4r1/tags-after.txt" | while read -r t id; do grep -q "^$t " "$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt" || run "docker image rm $t  # new tag from this run"; done
  run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/out-u4r1/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'tags restored'"
fi

echo '--- keep the six SBOMs and bundle.json in the tester scratch dir <scratch>/u4r1win-sboms, then delete the scratch bundle'
run "rm -rf \"$S/u4r1win-sboms\" && mkdir \"$S/u4r1win-sboms\" && cp -- \"$OUT\"/app/sboms/*.spdx.json \"$OUT/bundle.json\" \"$S/u4r1win-sboms/\" && (cd \"$S/u4r1win-sboms\" && sha256sum *.spdx.json)"
run "rm -rf -- \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\""
echo "[end 3b]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/out-u4r1/03b-addendum.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# 3b addendum (tester): the expected-count compare in 3b used a sed pattern anchored on ': <n>$', but each header line
# ends with the by-type breakdown, e.g. '(deb: 18, generic: 1)', so sed left the lines unchanged and diff exited 1. A
# tester-script bug, not a product result. Re-run here with the pattern fixed, on the same raw bundle.sh output
# (03b-bundle-raw.txt, written by the 3b tee and not touched since).
S=<scratch>
O="$S/out-u4r1"; RAW="$O/03b-bundle-raw.txt"; export S O RAW
red() { sed "s#$S#<scratch>#g"; }
run() { echo "\$ $1" | red; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
run 'sha256sum "$RAW"; stat -c "%s bytes, modified %y  %n" "$RAW"'
run 'cat "$O/expected-counts.txt"  # written by 3b'
run 'grep -E "^sbom_check: [a-z0-9-]+: unlicensed packages" "$RAW" | sed -E "s/^sbom_check: ([a-z0-9-]+): unlicensed packages \(no licence, NOASSERTION or unidentified text\): ([0-9]+)( \(.*\))?$/\1 \2/" | sort > "$O/printed-counts.txt"; cat "$O/printed-counts.txt"; diff "$O/expected-counts.txt" "$O/printed-counts.txt" && echo "printed counts == expected (ke-app 19, ke-gateway 0, neo4j 28, phoenix 35, postgres 25, valkey 1)"'
run 'echo "unable to get filesystem cache, bundle.sh run: $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$RAW"); base SBOM run (3b-drift): $(grep -c "unable to get filesystem cache" "$O/03b-drift-sbom-raw.txt")"'
echo '[end 3b addendum]'

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/recount_unlicensed.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4 Windows proof): recounts the unlicensed report of each SBOM a real bundle.sh run wrote,
with scripts/sbom_check.py loaded by path (never imported from the repo's package path). argv: <sbom_check.py> <bundle dir>."""
import importlib.util, json, pathlib, sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", sys.argv[1])
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
out = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
for name in ("ke-app", "ke-gateway", "neo4j", "phoenix", "postgres", "valkey"):
    doc = json.loads((out / "app" / "sboms" / f"{name}.spdx.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    r = m.unlicensed_report(doc)
    print(f"{name}: {r['count']} {dict(sorted(r['by_type'].items()))}")

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/drift_inputs.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U4 Windows proof, informational): what `sbom_check.py drift` compares, recomputed on the host with
scripts/sbom_check.py loaded by path. argv: <sbom_check.py> <ke-app SBOM> <base SBOM> <tests/test_notice_ke_app.py>."""
import importlib.util, json, sys

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", sys.argv[1])
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
a = json.load(open(sys.argv[2], encoding="utf-8"))
b = json.load(open(sys.argv[3], encoding="utf-8"))
added = m.added_packages(open(sys.argv[4], encoding="utf-8").read())
d = sorted(set(m._named(a, "deb")) - set(m._named(b, "deb")))
print("Debian packages ke-app adds to its base:", d)
print("ADDED_PACKAGES (tests/test_notice_ke_app.py, by ast):", sorted(added))
print("equal:", set(d) == set(added))
print("base SBOM: deb", len(m._named(b, "deb")), "pypi", len(m._named(b, "pypi")), "generic", sorted(m._named(b, "generic")))
print("ke-app libpq5:", sorted(m._named(a, "deb").get("libpq5", [])))
print("ke-app psycopg-c:", sorted(m._named(a, "pypi").get("psycopg-c", [])), "psycopg-binary present:", "psycopg-binary" in m._named(a, "pypi"))
print("ke-app pip present:", "pip" in m._named(a, "pypi"))
print("ke-app CPython (pkg:generic/python):", sorted(m._named(a, "generic").get("python", [])))
